### [190] [Same Geometry, Opposite Noise: Transformer Magnitude Representations Lack Scalar Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04469)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Transformer语言模型在处理数值大小时表现出与生物系统相反的表征变异性模式：变异性随数值增大而减小，表明分布式学习不足以产生标量变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验transformer语言模型是否表现出标量变异性（即噪声随数量级成比例增加），这是生物数量系统的一个典型特征。

Method: 通过分析三个7-8B参数的语言模型（Llama-3-8B-Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3、Llama-3-8B-Base）中26个数值大小在不同载体句子下的隐藏状态表征离散性，来研究其变异性与数值大小的关系。

Result: 发现表征变异性随数值增大而降低（标度指数α ≈ -0.19），该现象在全维度空间和句子身份校正后依然存在；且这一反标量模式在数量轴上比正交维度强3-5倍，语料库频率能很好地预测各数值的变异性（rho = .84）。

Conclusion: 仅靠分布学习不足以产生标量变异性；transformer虽能复现对数压缩的数量几何结构，但未再现生物系统中恒定变异系数的噪声特征。

Abstract: Scalar variability -- the finding that representational noise scales proportionally with magnitude, producing a constant coefficient of variation -- is a hallmark of biological magnitude systems. We tested whether transformer language models exhibit this property by analysing the dispersion of hidden-state representations across carrier sentences for 26 numerical magnitudes in three 7-8B parameter models (Llama-3-8B-Instruct, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, Llama-3-8B-Base; data from Cacioli, 2026). We found the opposite: representational variability decreased with magnitude along the magnitude axis (scaling exponent alpha approx -0.19; 0/16 primary layers with alpha > 0, all three models). The negative sign was consistent in full-dimensional space (alpha approx -0.04) and after sentence-identity correction (alpha approx -0.007). The anti-scalar pattern was 3-5x stronger along the magnitude axis than orthogonal dimensions, and corpus frequency strongly predicted per-magnitude variability (rho = .84). These results demonstrate that distributional learning alone is insufficient to produce scalar variability: transformers reproduce log-compressive magnitude geometry but not the constant-CV noise signature observed in biological systems.

</details>


### [191] [CommonMorph: Participatory Morphological Documentation Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04515)
*Aso Mahmudi,Sina Ahmadi,Kemal Kurniawan,Rico Sennrich,Eduard Hovy,Ekaterina Vylomova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为CommonMorph的开源平台，旨在通过专家定义、贡献者采集和社区验证的三层次方法，加速形态数据的收集与标注，特别支持低资源语言，并兼容多种形态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形态数据的收集和标注面临资源消耗大、专业要求高的问题，尤其对低资源语言而言更具挑战，因此需要一个高效、协作的技术平台来促进语言多样性保护。

Method: 提出CommonMorph平台，采用三层架构：专家语言学定义、贡献者语料采集、社区验证；结合主动学习、自动标注建议及跨语言资源迁移工具，支持UniMorph格式输出。

Result: 该平台减少了人工工作量，提升了数据采集效率，支持融合型、黏着型和词根-词缀型等多种形态系统，具备良好的可访问性和与NLP工具的互操作性。

Conclusion: CommonMorph为形态数据的协作式采集提供了可复制的技术模型，有助于推动低资源语言的数字化保存与自然语言处理应用。

Abstract: Collecting and annotating morphological data present significant challenges, requiring linguistic expertise, methodological rigour, and substantial resources. These barriers are particularly acute for low-resource languages and varieties. To accelerate this process, we introduce \texttt{CommonMorph}, a comprehensive platform that streamlines morphological data collection development through a three-tiered approach: expert linguistic definition, contributor elicitation, and community validation. The platform minimises manual work by incorporating active learning, annotation suggestions, and tools to import and adapt materials from related languages. It accommodates diverse morphological systems, including fusional, agglutinative, and root-and-pattern morphologies. Its open-source design and UniMorph-compatible outputs ensure accessibility and interoperability with NLP tools. Our platform is accessible at https://common-morph.com, offering a replicable model for preserving linguistic diversity through collaborative technology.

</details>


### [192] [Formal Constraints on Dependency Syntax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04542)
*Gómez-Rodríguez,Carlos,Alemany-Puig,Lluís*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了依存句法中用于描述真实语言现象的树结构约束，重点分析了投影性（projectivity）的局限性，并综述了为在语言准确性与结构灵活性之间取得平衡而提出的一系列替代约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于完全自由的依存结构过于宽松，而投影性约束又过于严格，难以刻画灵活语序语言中的语言现象，因此需要寻找更符合实际语言使用的中间约束条件。

Method: 通过综述和比较多种已提出的依存树约束条件，分析其在语言描写准确性、解析效率和人类语言处理等方面的优劣。

Result: 多种非投影但受限的依存结构约束被提出并评估，旨在更好地平衡语言现象的覆盖能力与计算可行性。

Conclusion: 未来的研究应继续探索既能反映语言多样性又能保持计算效率的依存结构约束。

Abstract: Dependency syntax represents the structure of a sentence as a tree composed of dependencies, i.e., directed relations between lexical units. While in its more general form any such tree is allowed, in practice many are not plausible or are very infrequent in attested language. This has motivated a search for constraints characterizing subsets of trees that better fit real linguistic phenomena, providing a more accurate linguistic description, faster parsing or insights on language evolution and human processing. Projectivity is the most well-studied such constraint, but it has been shown to be too restrictive to represent some linguistic phenomena, especially in flexible-word-order languages. Thus, a variety of constraints have been proposed to seek a realistic middle ground between the limitations of projectivity and the excessive leniency of unrestricted dependency structures.

</details>


### [193] [PassiveQA: A Three-Action Framework for Epistemically Calibrated Question Answering via Supervised Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04565)
*Madhav S Baidya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PassiveQA框架，通过监督微调使大语言模型在信息不足时具备认知意识，能够选择回答、请求澄清或 abstain，从而减少幻觉并提升问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中用户查询常不完整或模糊，现有大语言模型和RAG系统缺乏对信息充分性的判断能力，容易产生过度自信或幻觉回应。

Method: 提出PassiveQA三动作框架，结合结构化信息状态表示、知识图谱增强上下文，以及经过监督微调的决策规划器，显式建模缺失变量与决策逻辑。

Result: 在多个问答数据集上实验表明，该方法在计算受限训练下显著提升macro F1和放弃召回率，同时降低幻觉率。

Conclusion: 认知层面的决策能力需在训练中学习，而非仅在推理时强制实现，被动式决策机制更适应不完整信息环境。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance in question answering and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), yet they implicitly assume that user queries are fully specified and answerable. In real-world settings, queries are often incomplete, ambiguous, or missing critical variables, leading models to produce overconfident or hallucinated responses.
  In this work, we study decision-aware query resolution under incomplete information, where a model must determine whether to Answer, Ask for clarification, or Abstain. We show that standard and enhanced RAG systems do not reliably exhibit such epistemic awareness, defaulting to answer generation even when information is insufficient.
  To address this, we propose PassiveQA, a three-action framework that aligns model behaviour with information sufficiency through supervised finetuning. Our approach integrates structured information-state representations, knowledge graph-grounded context, and a finetuned planner that explicitly models missing variables and decision reasoning.
  Experiments across multiple QA datasets show that the finetuned planner achieves significant improvements in macro F1 and abstention recall while reducing hallucination rates, under a compute-constrained training regime.
  These results provide strong empirical evidence that epistemic decision-making must be learned during training rather than imposed at inference time.

</details>


### [194] [Benchmarking Multilingual Speech Models on Pashto: Zero-Shot ASR, Script Failure, and Cross-Domain Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04598)
*Hanif Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了首次在公开的普什图语数据上进行的多模型可重复评估，涵盖了零样本自动语音识别（ASR）、脚本级失败和跨域评估。研究发现现有模型在零样本设置下表现不佳，且存在严重的脚本输出错误问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普什图语虽有大量使用者，但在多语言自动语音识别领域缺乏公开基准测试。为了推动对该语言的支持和技术进步，需要系统评估当前模型的表现并识别存在的问题。

Method: 使用FLEURS普什图语测试集和过滤后的Common Voice~24子集，对包括Whisper系列、MMS-1B、SeamlessM4T-v2-large和OmniASR-CTC-300M在内的十种模型进行了零样本ASR评估；通过语言识别审计检查脚本输出保真度；并对五个微调后的普什图语ASR模型进行跨域性能评估。

Result: 零样本设置中，Whisper模型的词错误率（WER）从90%到297%不等，其中medium模型在Common Voice~24上达到461%，显示出解码循环问题；SeamlessM4T在Common Voice~24上取得39.7% WER的最佳结果；MMS-1B在FLEURS上为43.8%。语言识别审计显示，Whisper模型几乎不生成普什图文脚本输出，而MMS-1B、SeamlessM4T和OmniASR均超过93%的保真度。跨域评估中，已发表的14% WER在分布外数据下降至32.5%-59%，仅一个增强模型在两个数据集上实现35.1%且无退化。错误分析表明，普什图语特有的音素是主要错误来源。

Conclusion: 当前主流ASR模型在普什图语上的零样本和跨域表现严重不足，尤其存在脚本输出失败的问题，仅依赖WER无法反映真实性能。研究提出了五个阻碍进展的结构性问题，并列出了五个优先研究方向以促进未来工作。

Abstract: Pashto is spoken by approximately 60--80 million people but has no published benchmarks for multilingual automatic speech recognition (ASR) on any shared public test set. This paper reports the first reproducible multi-model evaluation on public Pashto data, covering zero-shot ASR, script-level failure, and cross-domain evaluation of fine-tuned models. For zero-shot ASR, ten models (all seven Whisper sizes, MMS-1B, SeamlessM4T-v2-large, and OmniASR-CTC-300M) are evaluated on the FLEURS Pashto test set and a filtered Common Voice~24 subset; zero-shot Whisper WER ranges from 90% to 297%, with the medium model collapsing to 461% on Common Voice~24 consistent with decoder looping. SeamlessM4T achieves 39.7% WER on Common Voice~24 (the best zero-shot result reported to date, as of submission); MMS-1B achieves 43.8% on FLEURS. For script failure, a language-identification audit shows that no Whisper model produces Pashto-script output in more than 0.8% of utterances, while MMS-1B, SeamlessM4T, and OmniASR each exceed 93% Pashto-script fidelity; WER alone does not reveal this failure, since a model generating Arabic-script output on Pashto audio has not achieved ASR in any interpretable sense. For cross-domain evaluation, five fine-tuned Pashto ASR models are evaluated on both test sets: published WER figures of 14% degrade to 32.5--59% on out-of-distribution sets, while one augmented model achieves 35.1% on both sets with zero cross-domain degradation. Character-class error stratification confirms that Pashto-unique phonemes (the retroflex series and lateral fricatives) account for disproportionate error mass. All evaluations cover read speech only. Five structural impediments to cumulative progress are identified and five ordered research priorities are argued.

</details>


### [195] [IDIOLEX: Unified and Continuous Representations for Idiolectal and Stylistic Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04704)
*Anjali Kantharuban,Aarohi Srivastava,Fahim Faisal,Orevaoghene Ahia,Antonios Anastasopoulos,David Chiang,Yulia Tsvetkov,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的句子表示方法，专注于捕捉句子的风格和方言特征（即idiolectal表示学习），而非语义内容。通过IDIOLEX框架，结合句子来源信息和语言学特征，实现了对阿拉伯语和西班牙语方言的有效建模，并验证了其在跨领域分析与分类中的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子表示方法主要关注句子的语义内容，而忽略了表达方式（如风格和方言）的重要性。然而，后者在许多应用中至关重要，因此需要一种能够分离并捕捉语言风格和方言特征的表示方法。

Method: 提出了IDIOLEX框架，通过结合句子的来源（provenance）监督信号与句子内容的语言学特征，联合建模个体和群体层面的语言变异，从而学习连续的句子风格和方言表示。

Result: 在阿拉伯语和西班牙语的方言数据上验证了该方法的有效性，学习到的表示能够捕捉有意义的语言变异，并在不同领域间实现迁移；同时可用于语言模型的风格对齐训练目标。

Conclusion: 联合建模个体与群体层面的语言变异为idiolect研究提供了有效视角，所提方法有助于提升下游任务中对风格差异的敏感性，例如构建多样化和可访问的大语言模型。

Abstract: Existing sentence representations primarily encode what a sentence says, rather than how it is expressed, even though the latter is important for many applications. In contrast, we develop sentence representations that capture style and dialect, decoupled from semantic content. We call this the task of idiolectal representation learning. We introduce IDIOLEX, a framework for training models that combines supervision from a sentence's provenance with linguistic features of a sentence's content, to learn a continuous representation of each sentence's style and dialect. We evaluate the approach on dialects of both Arabic and Spanish. The learned representations capture meaningful variation and transfer across domains for analysis and classification. We further explore the use of these representations as training objectives for stylistically aligning language models. Our results suggest that jointly modeling individual and community-level variation provides a useful perspective for studying idiolect and supports downstream applications requiring sensitivity to stylistic differences, such as developing diverse and accessible LLMs.

</details>


### [196] [BiST: A Gold Standard Bangla-English Bilingual Corpus for Sentence Structure and Tense Classification with Inter-Annotator Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04708)
*Abdullah Al Shafi,Swapnil Kundu Argha,M. A. Moyeen,Abdul Muntakim,Shoumik Barman Polok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个高质量的孟加拉语-英语双语语料库BiST，用于句子级语法分类，涵盖句法结构和时态两个维度，并展示了其在多语言自然语言处理中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低资源语言（特别是孟加拉语）中高质量双语资源匮乏的问题，推动多语言NLP发展。

Method: 从开源百科和自然对话文本中收集数据，进行系统预处理和自动语言识别，由三位独立标注者进行标注，并通过Fleiss Kappa评估标注一致性。

Result: 构建了包含30,534个句子的BiST语料库，其中英语17,465句，孟加拉语13,069句；标注一致性κ值分别为0.82（结构）和0.88（时态）；实验表明基于语言特定表示的双编码器模型优于强大多语言编码器。

Conclusion: BiST为双语语法建模提供了统一资源，支持受控文本生成、自动反馈生成和跨语言表示学习等任务，促进基于语言学的多语言研究。

Abstract: High-quality bilingual resources remain a critical bottleneck for advancing multilingual NLP in low-resource settings, particularly for Bangla. To mitigate this gap, we introduce BiST, a rigorously curated Bangla-English corpus for sentence-level grammatical classification, annotated across two fundamental dimensions: syntactic structure (Simple, Complex, Compound, Complex-Compound) and tense (Present, Past, Future). The corpus is compiled from open-licensed encyclopedic sources and naturally composed conversational text, followed by systematic preprocessing and automated language identification, resulting in 30,534 sentences, including 17,465 English and 13,069 Bangla instances. Annotation quality is ensured through a multi-stage framework with three independent annotators and dimension-wise Fleiss Kappa ($κ$) agreement, yielding reliable and reproducible labels with $κ$ values of 0.82 and 0.88 for structural and temporal annotation, respectively. Statistical analyses demonstrate realistic structural and temporal distributions, while baseline evaluations show that dual-encoder architectures leveraging complementary language-specific representations consistently outperform strong multilingual encoders. Beyond benchmarking, BiST provides explicit linguistic supervision that supports grammatical modeling tasks, including controlled text generation, automated feedback generation, and cross-lingual representation learning. The corpus establishes a unified resource for bilingual grammatical modeling and facilitates linguistically grounded multilingual research.

</details>


### [197] [What Makes Good Multilingual Reasoning? Disentangling Reasoning Traces with Measurable Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04720)
*Dayeon Ki,Kevin Duh,Marine Carpuat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了将英语推理模式直接应用于多语言场景的假设，通过定义可测量的推理特征并分析其在不同语言中的表现，发现这些特征与准确性的关联因语言而异，甚至可能出现反转，表明需要适应性目标以支持语言特定的推理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型推理模型在非英语语言上的表现仍显著落后于英语，且多数工作假定只需让其他语言的推理趋同于英语即可弥补差距。本研究旨在探究多语言环境下有效推理的本质特征，并评估源自英语的推理特征是否真正有助于其他语言的推理性能。

Method: 定义了一套涵盖多语言对齐、推理步骤和推理流程的可量化推理特征，使用逻辑回归分析各特征与答案准确性之间的关联；训练稀疏自编码器从多语言推理轨迹中自动发现潜在的推理概念；并将这些特征用作测试时的选择策略，检验其引导模型实现更强多语言推理的能力。

Result: 在两个数学推理基准、四个大型推理模型和十种语言上的实验表明，大多数推理特征与准确性正相关，但相关性强弱在不同语言间差异显著，某些情况下甚至出现负相关或关系反转。稀疏自编码器成功发现了超越人工定义特征的潜在推理概念。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了以英语为中心的奖励机制设计，表明应采用适应性目标来容纳语言特有的推理模式，对多语言评测基准和奖励函数的设计具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) still exhibit large performance gaps between English and other languages, yet much current work assumes these gaps can be closed simply by making reasoning in every language resemble English reasoning. This work challenges this assumption by asking instead: what actually characterizes effective reasoning in multilingual settings, and to what extent do English-derived reasoning features genuinely help in other languages? We first define a suite of measurable reasoning features spanning multilingual alignment, reasoning step, and reasoning flow aspects of reasoning traces, and use logistic regression to quantify how each feature associates with final answer accuracy. We further train sparse autoencoders over multilingual traces to automatically discover latent reasoning concepts that instantiate or extend these features. Finally, we use the features as test-time selection policies to examine whether they can steer models toward stronger multilingual reasoning. Across two mathematical reasoning benchmarks, four LRMs, and 10 languages, we find that most features are positively associated with accuracy, but the strength of association varies considerably across languages and can even reverse in some. Our findings challenge English-centric reward designs and point toward adaptive objectives that accommodate language-specific reasoning patterns, with concrete implications for multilingual benchmark and reward design.

</details>


### [198] [Individual and Combined Effects of English as a Second Language and Typos on LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04723)
*Serena Liu,Yutong Yang,Prisha Sheth,Weixuan Dong,Mingjiao Diao,Xinru Zhu,Nikhil Banga,Oscar Melendez,Arnav Sharma,Minda Zhao,Marina Lin,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究结合英语作为第二语言（ESL）变体和拼写错误，评估大语言模型在真实输入下的性能，发现两者共同作用导致比单独因素更显著的性能下降，尤其在封闭式任务中更为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型主要在标准英语数据上训练，而现实中许多用户以非母语英语并带拼写错误的方式输入，现有研究却多将ESL变体和拼写错误分开分析，因此需要综合评估二者对模型性能的影响。

Method: 采用Trans-EnV框架生成八种ESL变体，并使用MulTypo工具注入低、中、高三类拼写错误水平，系统测试大语言模型在不同组合下的表现。

Result: ESL变体与拼写错误共同存在时性能下降更严重，但效应非简单叠加；封闭式任务中趋势一致，开放式任务结果较不一致。

Conclusion: 仅在干净的标准英语上评估模型会高估其实际表现，孤立地研究ESL或拼写错误不足以反映真实场景中的模型行为。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used globally, and because much of their training data is in English, they typically perform best on English inputs. As a result, many non-native English speakers interact with them in English as a second language (ESL), and these inputs often contain typographical errors. Prior work has largely studied the effects of ESL variation and typographical errors separately, even though they often co-occur in real-world use. In this study, we use the Trans-EnV framework to transform standard English inputs into eight ESL variants and apply MulTypo to inject typos at three levels: low, moderate, and severe. We find that combining ESL variation and typos generally leads to larger performance drops than either factor alone, though the combined effect is not simply additive. This pattern is clearest on closed-ended tasks, where performance degradation can be characterized more consistently across ESL variants and typo levels, while results on open-ended tasks are more mixed. Overall, these findings suggest that evaluations on clean standard English may overestimate real-world model performance, and that evaluating ESL variation and typographical errors in isolation does not fully capture model behavior in realistic settings.

</details>


### [199] [Metaphors We Compute By: A Computational Audit of Cultural Translation vs. Thinking in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04732)
*Yuan Chang,Jiaming Qu,Zhu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）是否具备文化感知推理能力，通过跨五个文化背景的隐喻生成任务实验，发现模型存在刻板印象和西方中心主义倾向，表明仅通过提示赋予文化身份无法保证真正的文化内嵌推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分语言能力与文化推理能力，探究大语言模型在多大程度上能够进行真正基于文化的推理，而非仅仅进行语言翻译或表面本地化。

Method: 设计了一个涵盖五种文化背景和多个抽象概念的隐喻生成任务，对大语言模型输出的隐喻内容进行实证分析，评估其文化多样性和潜在偏见。

Result: 模型在某些文化背景下表现出刻板印象化的隐喻使用，并倾向于采用西方默认的认知框架，即使在非西方文化提示下也未能完全摆脱该框架。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型在文化感知推理方面存在局限，仅靠文化身份提示不足以实现真正文化多元的创造性协作，需进一步改进模型训练与评估方式以提升文化包容性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often described as multilingual because they can understand and respond in many languages. However, speaking a language is not the same as reasoning within a culture. This distinction motivates a critical question: do LLMs truly conduct culture-aware reasoning? This paper presents a preliminary computational audit of cultural inclusivity in a creative writing task. We empirically examine whether LLMs act as culturally diverse creative partners or merely as cultural translators that leverage a dominant conceptual framework with localized expressions. Using a metaphor generation task spanning five cultural settings and several abstract concepts as a case study, we find that the model exhibits stereotyped metaphor usage for certain settings, as well as Western defaultism. These findings suggest that merely prompting an LLM with a cultural identity does not guarantee culturally grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [200] [Lighting Up or Dimming Down? Exploring Dark Patterns of LLMs in Co-Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04735)
*Zhu Li,Jiaming Qu,Yuan Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型作为写作助手时可能出现的五种“黑暗模式”，这些行为可能抑制或扭曲人类的创造性过程，初步结果显示讨好性行为普遍存在，而锚定效应则与文学形式有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型在协作创作中对人类创造力的影响，特别是识别可能抑制创造性表达的隐性行为模式。

Method: 通过一系列受控会话，将大型语言模型提示为不同文学形式和主题下的写作助手，分析其生成回应中五种黑暗模式的出现频率。

Result: 初步结果表明，讨好性（Sycophancy）在91.7%的情况下普遍存在，尤其在敏感话题中；锚定效应（Anchoring）则依赖于文学形式，在寓言故事中最常出现。

Conclusion: 这些黑暗模式可能是安全对齐的副产品，无意中限制了创意探索，研究提出了支持创造性写作的AI系统设计考虑。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly acting as collaborative writing partners, raising questions about their impact on human agency. In this exploratory work, we investigate five "dark patterns" in human-AI co-creativity -- subtle model behaviors that can suppress or distort the creative process: Sycophancy, Tone Policing, Moralizing, Loop of Death, and Anchoring. Through a series of controlled sessions where LLMs are prompted as writing assistants across diverse literary forms and themes, we analyze the prevalence of these behaviors in generated responses. Our preliminary results suggest that Sycophancy is nearly ubiquitous (91.7% of cases), particularly in sensitive topics, while Anchoring appears to be dependent on literary forms, surfacing most frequently in folktales. This study indicates that these dark patterns, often byproducts of safety alignment, may inadvertently narrow creative exploration and proposes design considerations for AI systems that effectively support creative writing.

</details>


### [201] [Hallucination Basins: A Dynamic Framework for Understanding and Controlling LLM Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04743)
*Kalyan Cherukuri,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于几何动力系统框架的分析方法，揭示大语言模型幻觉源于任务依赖的潜在空间盆地结构，并通过几何感知引导减少幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会产生事实性错误的幻觉问题，需要从动力学角度理解其生成机制。

Method: 利用自回归隐藏状态轨迹分析多个开源模型和基准上的潜空间结构，提出任务复杂度与多盆地定理，形式化描述变换器中的盆地形成机制。

Result: 发现不同任务下潜空间的可分性差异显著：事实型任务盆地分离清晰，而摘要和易产生误解的任务则不稳定且常重叠；几何感知引导可有效降低幻觉概率。

Conclusion: 幻觉源于任务依赖的潜空间盆地结构，通过几何分析可实现无需重训练的幻觉抑制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate: they produce fluent outputs that are factually incorrect. We present a geometric dynamical systems framework in which hallucinations arise from task-dependent basin structure in latent space. Using autoregressive hidden-state trajectories across multiple open-source models and benchmarks, we find that separability is strongly task-dependent rather than universal: factoid settings can show clearer basin separation, whereas summarization and misconception-heavy settings are typically less stable and often overlap. We formalize this behavior with task-complexity and multi-basin theorems, characterize basin emergence in L-layer transformers, and show that geometry-aware steering can reduce hallucination probability without retraining.

</details>


### [202] [HUKUKBERT: Domain-Specific Language Model for Turkish Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04790)
*Mehmet Utku Öztürk,Tansu Türkoğlu,Buse Buz-Yalug*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HukukBERT，这是首个针对土耳其法律领域的大规模语言模型，采用混合领域自适应预训练方法，在清洗后的18GB法律语料上训练，并在法律完形填空和法院文书结构分割任务中达到最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏特定领域的数据和模型，土耳其法律领域的自然语言处理研究相对滞后。尽管英语法律文本已有如LEGAL-BERT等成熟模型，但土耳其语尚无对应的大规模专用模型。

Method: 提出HukukBERT，使用包含Whole-Word Masking、Token Span Masking、Word Span Masking和关键词掩码的混合领域自适应预训练（DAPT）方法，基于18GB土耳其法律语料训练，并采用48K WordPiece分词器。

Result: 在新建的法律完形填空测试基准上，HukukBERT取得84.40%的Top-1准确率；在法院文书结构分割任务中达到92.8%的文档通过率，均优于现有模型。

Conclusion: HukukBERT是目前最全面的土耳其法律语言模型，显著提升了该领域NLP任务的性能，作者已公开发布该模型以促进未来研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in natural language processing (NLP) have increasingly enabled LegalTech applications, yet existing studies specific to Turkish law have still been limited due to the scarcity of domain-specific data and models. Although extensive models like LEGAL-BERT have been developed for English legal texts, the Turkish legal domain lacks a domain-specific high-volume counterpart. In this paper, we introduce HukukBERT, the most comprehensive legal language model for Turkish, trained on a 18 GB cleaned legal corpus using a hybrid Domain-Adaptive Pre-Training (DAPT) methodology integrating Whole-Word Masking, Token Span Masking, Word Span Masking, and targeted Keyword Masking. We systematically compared our 48K WordPiece tokenizer and DAPT approach against general-purpose and existing domain-specific Turkish models. Evaluated on a novel Legal Cloze Test benchmark -- a masked legal term prediction task designed for Turkish court decisions -- HukukBERT achieves state-of-the-art performance with 84.40\% Top-1 accuracy, substantially outperforming existing models. Furthermore, we evaluated HukukBERT in the downstream task of structural segmentation of official Turkish court decisions, where it achieves a 92.8\% document pass rate, establishing a new state-of-the-art. We release HukukBERT to support future research in Turkish legal NLP tasks, including recognition of named entities, prediction of judgment, and classification of legal documents.

</details>


### [203] [How Far Are We? Systematic Evaluation of LLMs vs. Human Experts in Mathematical Contest in Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04791)
*Yuhang Liu,Heyan Huang,Yizhe Yang,Hongyan Zhao,Zhizhuo Zeng,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向问题的分阶段评估框架，用于衡量大语言模型在数学建模竞赛中的端到端解决问题能力，发现当前模型在执行阶段存在显著缺陷，且该问题无法通过单纯扩大模型规模解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在真实世界复杂问题（如数学建模）中的端到端解决能力，现有评测方法不足以准确反映其各阶段表现。

Method: 构建基于专家验证标准的分阶段评估框架，并在中国研究生数学建模竞赛题目上比较自动评分与人工专家评分的一致性，进而系统分析大模型在不同建模阶段的表现。

Result: 该框架与人工评分具有高度一致性，优于现有评测方法；发现大模型在问题理解与建模阶段表现良好，但在求解、编码和结果分析等执行阶段表现不佳，错误会跨阶段传播，且模型规模增大未能弥补这一差距。

Conclusion: 仅靠模型扩展无法弥合大语言模型在执行阶段的能力短板，需引入更严格的规范、验证与纠错机制，以提升其在现实复杂任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on reasoning benchmarks, yet their ability to solve real-world problems requiring end-to-end workflows remains unclear. Mathematical modeling competitions provide a stringent testbed for evaluating such end-to-end problem-solving capability. We propose a problem-oriented, stage-wise evaluation framework that assesses LLM performance across modeling stages using expert-verified criteria. We validate the framework's reliability by comparing automatic scores with independent human expert judgments on problems from the China Postgraduate Mathematical Contest in Modeling, demonstrating substantially stronger alignment than existing evaluation schemes. Using this framework, we reveal a comprehension-execution gap in state-of-the-art LLMs: while they perform well in early stages such as problem identification and formulation, they exhibit persistent deficiencies in execution-oriented stages including model solving, code implementation, and result analysis. These gaps persist even with increased model scale. We further trace these failures to insufficient specification, missing verification, and lack of validation, with errors propagating across stages without correction. Our findings suggest that bridging this gap requires approaches beyond model scaling, offering insights for applying LLMs to complex real-world problem solving.

</details>


### [204] [LiveFact: A Dynamic, Time-Aware Benchmark for LLM-Driven Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04815)
*Cheng Xu,Changhong Jin,Yingjie Niu,Nan Yan,Yuke Mei,Shuhao Guan,Liming Chen,M-Tahar Kechadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LiveFact是一个持续更新的动态基准，用于评估大语言模型在信息随时间演变且不完整情况下的推理能力，揭示了现有模型在面对不确定性时的认知谦逊与显著的‘推理差距’。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的假新闻检测和事实核查基准多为静态，易受数据污染影响，且无法有效评估模型在时间不确定性和动态信息下的推理能力。因此需要一个能模拟现实世界信息混乱状态的动态评估框架。

Method: 提出LiveFact，一个持续更新的基准，采用动态、有时序性的证据集，设计分类模式（最终验证）和推理模式（基于证据的推理）两种评估方式，并包含专门组件监测基准数据污染（BDC）。

Result: 对22个大语言模型的测试显示，开源的混合专家模型（如Qwen3-235B-A22B）已达到或超过专有最先进系统的性能；更重要的是发现了‘推理差距’：能力强的模型能在早期数据片段中识别出无法验证的主张，表现出认知谦逊。

Conclusion: LiveFact建立了一个可持续的标准，能够更真实地评估具备时间感知能力的人工智能验证系统，推动模型向更稳健、更可靠的推理发展。

Abstract: The rapid development of Large Language Models (LLMs) has transformed fake news detection and fact-checking tasks from simple classification to complex reasoning. However, evaluation frameworks have not kept pace. Current benchmarks are static, making them vulnerable to benchmark data contamination (BDC) and ineffective at assessing reasoning under temporal uncertainty. To address this, we introduce LiveFact a continuously updated benchmark that simulates the real-world "fog of war" in misinformation detection. LiveFact uses dynamic, temporal evidence sets to evaluate models on their ability to reason with evolving, incomplete information rather than on memorized knowledge. We propose a dual-mode evaluation: Classification Mode for final verification and Inference Mode for evidence-based reasoning, along with a component to monitor BDC explicitly. Tests with 22 LLMs show that open-source Mixture-of-Experts models, such as Qwen3-235B-A22B, now match or outperform proprietary state-of-the-art systems. More importantly, our analysis finds a significant "reasoning gap." Capable models exhibit epistemic humility by recognizing unverifiable claims in early data slices-an aspect traditional static benchmarks overlook. LiveFact sets a sustainable standard for evaluating robust, temporally aware AI verification.

</details>


### [205] [Plausibility as Commonsense Reasoning: Humans Succeed, Large Language Models Do not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04825)
*Sercan Karakaş*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型在土耳其语前置关系从句依附歧义中是否像人类一样以结构敏感的方式利用事件合理性的信息。实验发现人类受合理性影响明显，而现有语言模型对合理性的利用较弱、不稳定或方向相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验大型语言模型是否真正像人类一样将世界知识与句法结构结合，在歧义解析中表现出结构敏感性。

Method: 构建保持句法结构不变但事件合理性不同的土耳其语歧义句，在人类受试者中进行快速强制选择理解实验，并用独立的合理性评分验证刺激材料；对土耳其语及多语言大模型采用基于偏好匹配的高/低依附延续的平均每词元对数概率比较方法进行平行评估。

Result: 人类表现出强烈且方向正确的合理性效应，而各类大语言模型的合理性驱动变化较弱、不稳定或出现反转。

Conclusion: 当前测试的语言模型未能像人类判断那样可靠地利用合理性信息来引导依附偏好，表明其在整合世界知识与句法结构方面仍存在局限，同时也凸显土耳其语关系从句依附作为跨语言诊断工具的价值。

Abstract: Large language models achieve strong performance on many language tasks, yet it remains unclear whether they integrate world knowledge with syntactic structure in a human-like, structure-sensitive way during ambiguity resolution. We test this question in Turkish prenominal relative-clause attachment ambiguities, where the same surface string permits high attachment (HA) or low attachment (LA). We construct ambiguous items that keep the syntactic configuration fixed and ensure both parses remain pragmatically possible, while graded event plausibility selectively favors High Attachment vs.\ Low Attachment. The contrasts are validated with independent norming ratings. In a speeded forced-choice comprehension experiment, humans show a large, correctly directed plausibility effect. We then evaluate Turkish and multilingual LLMs in a parallel preference-based setup that compares matched HA/LA continuations via mean per-token log-probability. Across models, plausibility-driven shifts are weak, unstable, or reversed. The results suggest that, in the tested models, plausibility information does not guide attachment preferences as reliably as it does in human judgments, and they highlight Turkish RC attachment as a useful cross-linguistic diagnostic beyond broad benchmarks.

</details>


### [206] [MERIT: Multilingual Expert-Reward Informed Tuning for Chinese-Centric Low-Resource Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04839)
*Zhixiang Lu,Chong Zhang,Chenyu Xue,Angelos Stefanidis,Chong Li,Jionglong Su,Zhengyong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MERIT的统一翻译框架，用于改善中文与东南亚低资源语言之间的神经机器翻译，通过语言特定的前缀标记、监督微调和基于语义对齐奖励的群体相对策略优化，显著提升了翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏干净的平行语料库以及现有挖掘数据中的噪声问题，中文到东南亚低资源语言的神经机器翻译受到严重限制，导致翻译质量低下。

Method: 提出了MERIT框架，结合语言特定词元前缀（LTP）、监督微调（SFT）和一种新的由语义对齐奖励（SAR）引导的群体相对策略优化（GRPO）方法，并将传统的以英语为中心的评估基准转为以中文为中心的评估套件。

Result: 在低资源语言到中文的翻译任务中，MERIT框架通过有针对性的数据整理和奖励引导优化，显著优于单纯扩大模型规模的方法。

Conclusion: 针对低资源语言的翻译任务，数据质量和训练策略比模型规模更为重要，MERIT为中文中心的多语言翻译提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) from Chinese to low-resource Southeast Asian languages remains severely constrained by the extreme scarcity of clean parallel corpora and the pervasive noise in existing mined data. This chronic shortage not only impedes effective model training but also sustains a large performance gap with high-resource directions, leaving millions of speakers of languages such as Lao, Burmese, and Tagalog with persistently low-quality translation systems despite recent advances in large multilingual models. We introduce \textbf{M}ultilingual \textbf{E}xpert-\textbf{R}eward \textbf{I}nformed \textbf{T}uning (\textbf{MERIT}), a unified translation framework that transforms the traditional English-centric ALT benchmark into a Chinese-centric evaluation suite for five Southeast Asian low-resource languages (LRLs). Our framework combines language-specific token prefixing (LTP) with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and a novel group relative policy optimization (GRPO) guided by the semantic alignment reward (SAR). These results confirm that, in LRL{\textrightarrow}Chinese translation, targeted data curation and reward-guided optimization dramatically outperform mere model scaling.

</details>


### [207] [Do No Harm: Exposing Hidden Vulnerabilities of LLMs via Persona-based Client Simulation Attack in Psychological Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04842)
*Qingyang Xu,Yaling Shen,Stephanie Fong,Zimu Wang,Yiwen Jiang,Xiangyu Zhao,Jiahe Liu,Zhongxing Xu,Vincent Lee,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人格的客户模拟攻击（PCSA）框架，用于检测大型语言模型在心理健康治疗对话中的心理安全对齐漏洞，揭示了现有模型在共情与不当支持之间的界限模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的红队测试框架主要关注通用性危害或基于优化的攻击，忽视了心理健康领域中治疗性共情与不良验证之间的重要区别，因此需要一种更贴近真实咨询场景的评估方法。

Method: 提出PCSA框架，通过构建具有连贯人格特征的虚拟客户对话来模拟真实心理咨询过程，并在七个通用和心理健康专用的大模型上进行测试，结合困惑度分析和人工评估来衡量生成对话的自然性和有效性。

Result: PCSA在暴露模型心理安全漏洞方面显著优于四种基线方法，能够生成更自然、真实的对话；实验发现当前大模型仍易受特定领域对抗策略影响，可能出现提供非授权医疗建议、强化妄想或隐含鼓励危险行为的问题。

Conclusion: PCSA为评估心理健康应用中的大模型安全性提供了新路径，突显了现有模型在高风险情境下对共情误用的风险，强调需加强领域特定的安全对齐机制。

Abstract: The increasing use of large language models (LLMs) in mental healthcare raises safety concerns in high-stakes therapeutic interactions. A key challenge is distinguishing therapeutic empathy from maladaptive validation, where supportive responses may inadvertently reinforce harmful beliefs or behaviors in multi-turn conversations. This risk is largely overlooked by existing red-teaming frameworks, which focus mainly on generic harms or optimization-based attacks. To address this gap, we introduce Personality-based Client Simulation Attack (PCSA), the first red-teaming framework that simulates clients in psychological counseling through coherent, persona-driven client dialogues to expose vulnerabilities in psychological safety alignment. Experiments on seven general and mental health-specialized LLMs show that PCSA substantially outperforms four competitive baselines. Perplexity analysis and human inspection further indicate that PCSA generates more natural and realistic dialogues. Our results reveal that current LLMs remain vulnerable to domain-specific adversarial tactics, providing unauthorized medical advice, reinforcing delusions, and implicitly encouraging risky actions.

</details>


### [208] [Rethinking Exploration in RLVR: From Entropy Regularization to Refinement via Bidirectional Entropy Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04894)
*Hengrui Gu,Xiaotian Han,Yujing Bian,Kaixiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的强化学习框架AsymGRPO，通过分解策略熵为“信息熵”和“虚假熵”，实现对正向与负向 rollout 的独立调控，从而提升大语言模型的探索效率与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于奖励验证的强化学习（RLVR）在大语言模型中存在“探索受限”问题，熵正则化方法因超参数敏感且增益有限而效果不佳，因此需要重新思考策略熵与探索之间的关系。

Method: 通过推导群组相对优势估计的参数化形式并分析熵动态，将策略熵分解为信息熵和虚假熵；提出AsymGRPO框架，显式解耦正向与负向rollout的调节过程，保留有益多样性并抑制噪声。

Result: 实验表明，AsymGRPO在多个任务上优于强基线方法，并能与现有熵正则化技术协同使用，有效提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 有效的探索不在于盲目增加熵，而在于精炼熵——保持信息熵、抑制虚假熵；AsymGRPO为大语言模型中的探索问题提供了新视角和有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, it faces a fundamental limitation termed \textit{restricted exploration}, where the policy rapidly converges to a narrow set of solutions. While entropy regularization is a popular approach used to sustain exploration, it often proves unreliable for LLMs, suffering from high hyperparameter sensitivity and yielding only marginal performance gains. Motivated by these inefficiencies, we propose to rethink the relationship between policy entropy and exploration. By deriving a parametric formulation of group-relative advantage estimation and analyzing entropy dynamics, we conceptually decompose policy entropy into \textit{informative entropy}, which preserves diverse solution paths, and \textit{spurious entropy}, which erodes reasoning patterns. Our analysis reveals that, in contrast to blind maximization, effective exploration requires \textit{entropy refinement}-a mechanism implicitly embedded in group-relative advantage estimation that sustains informative entropy on positive rollouts while suppressing spurious entropy on negative ones. Guided by this insight, we propose \textbf{AsymGRPO}, an exploratory framework that explicitly decouples the modulation of positive and negative rollouts. This allows for independent control over the preservation of informative entropy and the suppression of spurious noise. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AsymGRPO achieves superior performance compared to strong baselines and exhibits the potential to synergize with existing entropy regularization methods.

</details>


### [209] [TriAttention: Efficient Long Reasoning with Trigonometric KV Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04921)
*Weian Mao,Xi Lin,Wei Huang,Yuxin Xie,Tianfu Fu,Bohan Zhuang,Song Han,Yukang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TriAttention的KV缓存压缩方法，通过利用RoPE前空间中Q/K向量的集中特性与三角级数关系，实现更准确的键重要性估计，在保持推理精度的同时显著减少内存占用并提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力分数的KV缓存压缩方法在RoPE位置编码下因查询旋转导致代表性不足，难以稳定选择重要键，从而影响长上下文推理性能。

Method: 在RoPE前的空间中分析Q和K向量的集中现象，利用其固定的非零中心通过三角级数建模查询对特定距离键的偏好，并结合Q/K范数作为额外信号来评分键的重要性。

Result: 在AIME25任务上生成32K token时，TriAttention在保持完整注意力精度的同时实现了2.5倍吞吐提升或10.7倍KV内存压缩，而现有基线方法仅能达到约一半的精度。

Conclusion: TriAttention有效解决了RoPE带来的查询旋转问题，通过Q/K集中性和距离偏好建模实现了高效稳定的长序列推理，使大模型可在单个消费级GPU上部署长上下文应用。

Abstract: Extended reasoning in large language models (LLMs) creates severe KV cache memory bottlenecks. Leading KV cache compression methods estimate KV importance using attention scores from recent post-RoPE queries. However, queries rotate with position during RoPE, making representative queries very few, leading to poor top-key selection and unstable reasoning. To avoid this issue, we turn to the pre-RoPE space, where we observe that Q and K vectors are highly concentrated around fixed non-zero centers and remain stable across positions -- Q/K concentration. We show that this concentration causes queries to preferentially attend to keys at specific distances (e.g., nearest keys), with the centers determining which distances are preferred via a trigonometric series. Based on this, we propose TriAttention to estimate key importance by leveraging these centers. Via the trigonometric series, we use the distance preference characterized by these centers to score keys according to their positions, and also leverage Q/K norms as an additional signal for importance estimation. On AIME25 with 32K-token generation, TriAttention matches Full Attention reasoning accuracy while achieving 2.5x higher throughput or 10.7x KV memory reduction, whereas leading baselines achieve only about half the accuracy at the same efficiency. TriAttention enables OpenClaw deployment on a single consumer GPU, where long context would otherwise cause out-of-memory with Full Attention.

</details>


### [210] [Early Stopping for Large Reasoning Models via Confidence Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04930)
*Parsa Hosseini,Sumit Nawathe,Mahdi Salmani,Meisam Razaviyayn,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CoDE-Stop的早期停止方法，利用推理过程中中间答案的置信度动态来决定何时终止推理，无需额外训练且能有效减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在解决复杂问题时依赖长链式思维生成，但过长的推理过程会导致高昂的计算成本，并可能因过度思考而降低性能。关键挑战在于如何判断模型应在何时停止推理并输出最终答案。

Method: 通过研究推理过程中间答案的置信度，发现正确推理路径通常较早达到高置信度，而错误路径则表现出不稳定或不可靠的置信度动态。基于此，提出CoDE-Stop方法，利用置信度动态实现早期停止。

Result: 在多个推理和科学基准及不同模型上验证了CoDE-Stop的有效性，相比先前方法实现了更优的准确率-计算成本权衡，与标准全长推理相比总token使用量减少了25%-50%。

Conclusion: CoDE-Stop是一种高效、无需训练的早期停止策略，能够显著降低推理成本，同时提供对推理过程中置信度动态的深入理解。

Abstract: Large reasoning models rely on long chain-of-thought generation to solve complex problems, but extended reasoning often incurs substantial computational cost and can even degrade performance due to overthinking. A key challenge is determining when the model should stop reasoning and produce the final answer. In this work, we study the confidence of intermediate answers during reasoning and observe two characteristic behaviors: correct reasoning trajectories often reach high-confidence answers early, while incorrect rollouts tend to produce long, unproductive reasoning traces and exhibit less reliable confidence dynamics. Motivated by these observations, we propose CoDE-Stop (Confidence Dynamics Early Stop), an early stopping method that leverages the dynamics of intermediate answer confidence to decide when to terminate reasoning, requiring no additional training and easily integrating into existing models. We evaluate CoDE-Stop on diverse reasoning and science benchmarks across multiple models. Compared to prior early stopping methods, it achieves a more favorable accuracy-compute tradeoff and reduces total token usage by 25-50% compared to standard full-length reasoning. In addition, we provide analyses of confidence dynamics during reasoning, offering insights into how confidence changes in both correct and incorrect trajectories.

</details>


### [211] [Beyond the Final Actor: Modeling the Dual Roles of Creator and Editor for Fine-Grained LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04932)
*Yang Li,Qiang Sheng,Zhengjia Wang,Yehan Yang,Danding Wang,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种细粒度的LLM生成文本检测方法RACE，能够在四分类设置下有效区分创作者与编辑者的文本特征，通过修辞结构理论和基本话语单元特征提升检测精度，适用于精细化的LLM监管需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的二分类或三分类方法无法满足对LLM润色文本和人类化生成文本进行差异化监管的需求，缺乏细粒度的检测能力。

Method: 提出RACE方法，基于修辞结构理论构建逻辑图以捕捉创作者的底层结构，并在基本话语单元级别提取特征以刻画编辑者的风格，实现创建者与编辑者的建模分离。

Result: 实验表明，RACE在四类细粒度分类任务中优于12种基线方法，误报率低，能更准确识别不同类型的合成文本。

Conclusion: RACE为LLM生成文本提供了政策对齐的精细化检测方案，有助于实现更有针对性的内容监管。

Abstract: The misuse of large language models (LLMs) requires precise detection of synthetic text. Existing works mainly follow binary or ternary classification settings, which can only distinguish pure human/LLM text or collaborative text at best. This remains insufficient for the nuanced regulation, as the LLM-polished human text and humanized LLM text often trigger different policy consequences. In this paper, we explore fine-grained LLM-generated text detection under a rigorous four-class setting. To handle such complexities, we propose RACE (Rhetorical Analysis for Creator-Editor Modeling), a fine-grained detection method that characterizes the distinct signatures of creator and editor. Specifically, RACE utilizes Rhetorical Structure Theory to construct a logic graph for the creator's foundation while extracting Elementary Discourse Unit-level features for the editor's style. Experiments show that RACE outperforms 12 baselines in identifying fine-grained types with low false alarms, offering a policy-aligned solution for LLM regulation.

</details>


### [212] [LLMs-Healthcare : Current Applications and Challenges of Large Language Models in various Medical Specialties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.12882)
*Ummara Mumtaz,Awais Ahmed,Summaya Mumtaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了大型语言模型（LLMs）在医疗领域的最新进展，重点探讨其在癌症护理、皮肤病学、牙科、神经退行性疾病和心理健康等领域的诊断与治疗应用，并分析了LLM在多模态医疗数据处理中的挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs在自然语言处理方面的显著进步，探索其在医疗决策支持、疾病诊断和个性化治疗中的潜力具有重要意义，旨在推动智能医疗的发展并提升临床效率。

Method: 通过文献综述的方法，系统梳理LLMs在多个医学专科中的应用案例，分析其技术实现方式、数据处理策略及实际部署中的表现。

Result: LLMs在多种医疗场景中展现出强大的辅助能力，能够提高诊断准确性、优化治疗方案并改善患者管理，同时在处理文本、影像和电子健康记录等多源异构数据方面取得初步成效。

Conclusion: 尽管存在数据隐私、模型可解释性和临床验证等挑战，LLMs在医疗领域具有广阔前景，未来需加强跨学科合作以推动其安全、可靠地融入临床实践。

Abstract: We aim to present a comprehensive overview of the latest advancements in utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) within the healthcare sector, emphasizing their transformative impact across various medical domains. LLMs have become pivotal in supporting healthcare, including physicians, healthcare providers, and patients. Our review provides insight into the applications of Large Language Models (LLMs) in healthcare, specifically focusing on diagnostic and treatment-related functionalities. We shed light on how LLMs are applied in cancer care, dermatology, dental care, neurodegenerative disorders, and mental health, highlighting their innovative contributions to medical diagnostics and patient care. Throughout our analysis, we explore the challenges and opportunities associated with integrating LLMs in healthcare, recognizing their potential across various medical specialties despite existing limitations. Additionally, we offer an overview of handling diverse data types within the medical field.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Learning-Based Fault Detection for Legged Robots in Remote Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03397)
*Abriana Stewart-Height,Seema Jahagirdar,Nikolai Matni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离线学习的方法，利用本体感知传感器数据检测四足机器人单个肢体故障，并使控制器能够选择合适的三足步态以适应损伤后的形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在危险环境中执行任务时，无法像生物一样自主感知并适应肢体损伤，因此需要一种故障检测机制来提升其生存能力和任务连续性。

Method: 采用基于离线学习的方法，从本体感知传感器数据中识别单个肢体故障，并将结果输入控制器以切换至相应的三足步态。

Result: 成功实现了对单肢故障的检测，并能有效驱动控制器选择适合当前物理形态的三足运动模式。

Conclusion: 该方法提升了四足机器人在动态复杂环境中面对肢体损伤时的自主适应能力，有助于其在远程高风险任务中的可靠运行。

Abstract: Operations in hazardous environments put humans, animals, and machines at high risk for physically damaging consequences. In contrast to humans and animals, quadruped robots cannot naturally identify and adjust their locomotion to a severely debilitated limb. The ability to detect limb damage and adjust movement to a new physical morphology is the difference between survival and death for humans and animals. The same can be said for quadruped robots autonomously carrying out remote assignments in dynamic, complex settings. This work presents the development and implementation of an off-line learning-based method to detect single limb faults from proprioceptive sensor data in a quadrupedal robot. The aim of the fault detection technique is to provide the correct output for the controller to select the appropriate tripedal gait to use given the robot's current physical morphology.

</details>


### [214] [Diffusion Policy with Bayesian Expert Selection for Active Multi-Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03404)
*Haotian Xiang,Qin Lu,Yaakov Bar-Shalom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架的悲观、不确定性感知策略选择方法，用于扩散策略中的专家选择，以在主动多目标跟踪中平衡探索与利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略方法在去噪过程中隐式选择策略，缺乏对执行策略的不确定性量化，导致可能过度依赖不可靠的预测。

Method: 将专家选择建模为离线上下文_bandit_问题，采用多头变分贝叶斯最后一层（VBLL）模型预测各策略在当前信念状态下的期望跟踪性能，并结合下置信界（LCB）准则进行悲观策略选择，所选专家用于条件化扩散策略生成动作序列。

Result: 在模拟室内跟踪场景中的实验表明，该方法优于基础扩散策略及标准门控方法（如混合专家选择和确定性回归基线）。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过不确定性感知的悲观策略选择，有效提升了主动多目标跟踪中扩散策略的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Active multi-target tracking requires a mobile robot to balance exploration for undetected targets with exploitation of uncertain tracked ones. Diffusion policies have emerged as a powerful approach for capturing diverse behavioral strategies by learning action sequences from expert demonstrations. However, existing methods implicitly select among strategies through the denoising process, without uncertainty quantification over which strategy to execute. We formulate expert selection for diffusion policies as an offline contextual bandit problem and propose a Bayesian framework for pessimistic, uncertainty-aware strategy selection. A multi-head Variational Bayesian Last Layer (VBLL) model predicts the expected tracking performance of each expert strategy given the current belief state, providing both a point estimate and predictive uncertainty. Following the pessimism principle for offline decision-making, a Lower Confidence Bound (LCB) criterion then selects the expert whose worst-case predicted performance is best, avoiding overcommitment to experts with unreliable predictions. The selected expert conditions a diffusion policy to generate corresponding action sequences. Experiments on simulated indoor tracking scenarios demonstrate that our approach outperforms both the base diffusion policy and standard gating methods, including Mixture-of-Experts selection and deterministic regression baselines.

</details>


### [215] [Do Robots Need Body Language? Comparing Communication Modalities for Legible Motion Intent in Human-Shared Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03451)
*Jonathan Albert Cohen,Kye Shimizu,Allen Song,Vishnu Bharath,Kent Larson,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过在线视频实验，评估了表达性动作、灯光、文本和音频等不同信号方式对人类理解四足机器人（Boston Dynamics Spot）导航意图的影响，探讨了隐式与显式信号策略在预测准确性、信心和信任方面的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于共享空间中的机器人运动常难以被人类理解，而高自由度机器人产生的表达性运动会被人解读，因此有必要研究人们如何感知这些信号，以提升人机共处的安全与效率。

Method: 开展了一项在线视频实验，测试四种常见场景下，表达性动作、灯光、文本和音频等单一及多模态信号对人类预测机器人下一步行动的准确性、信心和信任的影响，并比较一致与冲突信号的效果。

Result: 发现表达性运动在某些情况下可媲美显式信号；多模态一致信号能提升可解释性；而冲突信号会降低用户的信心与信任。提供了隐式与显式信号策略相对有效性的初步证据。

Conclusion: 合理设计的表达性运动可作为有效的隐式通信手段，结合一致的多模态信号能显著提升人类对机器人意图的理解，对未来机器人交互设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Robots in shared spaces often move in ways that are difficult for people to interpret, placing the burden on humans to adapt. High-DoF robots exhibit motion that people read as expressive, intentionally or not, making it important to understand how such cues are perceived. We present an online video study evaluating how different signaling modalities, expressive motion, lights, text, and audio, shape people's ability to understand a quadruped robot's upcoming navigation actions (Boston Dynamics Spot). Across four common scenarios, we measure how each modality influences humans' (1) accuracy in predicting the robot's next navigation action, (2) confidence in that prediction, and (3) trust in the robot to act safely. The study tests how expressive motions compare to explicit channels, whether aligned multimodal cues enhance interpretability, and how conflicting cues affect user confidence and trust. We contribute initial evidence on the relative effectiveness of implicit versus explicit signaling strategies.

</details>


### [216] [Sim2Real-AD: A Modular Sim-to-Real Framework for Deploying VLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning in Real-World Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03497)
*Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Zihao Sheng,Boyue Wang,Junwei You,Yue Leng,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Sim2Real-AD的模块化框架，实现了在无真实世界强化学习训练数据的情况下，将CARLA仿真环境中训练的视觉语言模型（VLM）引导的强化学习策略零样本迁移至全尺寸真实车辆上，并在实际部署中取得了高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于仿真环境中的感知输入和动作语义通常无法在真实平台上直接获取，导致基于视觉语言模型的强化学习策略难以从仿真迁移到真实自动驾驶系统，本文旨在解决这一零样本仿真到现实迁移的核心挑战。

Method: 该方法构建了一个包含几何观测桥（GOB）、物理感知动作映射（PAM）、两阶段渐进训练（TPT）策略和实时部署流水线（RDP）的模块化框架：GOB将单目图像转换为仿真器兼容的鸟瞰图输入；PAM将策略输出映射为平台无关的物理控制指令；TPT分离动作空间与观测空间的迁移以提升稳定性；RDP实现端到端闭环部署。

Result: 仿真实验表明该框架能保持不同强化学习算法在多种奖励机制下的性能排序，并验证了各模块的有效性；在全尺寸福特E-Transit实车上的零样本部署实现了90%的跟车成功率、80%的避障成功率和75%的停车标志交互成功率。

Conclusion: Sim2Real-AD是首批实现CARLA训练的VLM引导强化学习策略在全尺寸真实车辆上零样本闭环部署的工作之一，无需任何真实世界的强化学习训练数据，展示了强大的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Deploying reinforcement learning policies trained in simulation to real autonomous vehicles remains a fundamental challenge, particularly for VLM-guided RL frameworks whose policies are typically learned with simulator-native observations and simulator-coupled action semantics that are unavailable on physical platforms. This paper presents Sim2Real-AD, a modular framework for zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of CARLA-trained VLM-guided RL policies to full-scale vehicles without any real-world RL training data. The framework decomposes the transfer problem into four components: a Geometric Observation Bridge (GOB) that converts monocular front-view images into simulator-compatible bird's-eye-view (BEV) observations, a Physics-Aware Action Mapping (PAM) that translates policy outputs into platform-agnostic physical commands, a Two-Phase Progressive Training (TPT) strategy that stabilizes adaptation by separating action-space and observation-space transfer, and a Real-time Deployment Pipeline (RDP) that integrates perception, policy inference, control conversion, and safety monitoring for closed-loop execution. Simulation experiments show that the framework preserves the relative performance ordering of representative RL algorithms across different reward paradigms and validate the contribution of each module. Zero-shot deployment on a full-scale Ford E-Transit achieves success rates of 90%, 80%, and 75% in car-following, obstacle avoidance, and stop-sign interaction scenarios, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, this study is among the first to demonstrate zero-shot closed-loop deployment of a CARLA-trained VLM-guided RL policy on a full-scale real vehicle without any real-world RL training data. The demo video and code are available at: https://zilin-huang.github.io/Sim2Real-AD-website/.

</details>


### [217] [HAD: Combining Hierarchical Diffusion with Metric-Decoupled RL for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03581)
*Wenhao Yao,Xinglong Sun,Zhenxin Li,Shiyi Lan,Zi Wang,Jose M. Alvarez,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自动驾驶的端到端规划框架HAD，采用分层扩散策略将规划过程分解为粗到精的步骤，并引入结构保持轨迹扩展和度量解耦策略优化，显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端规划方法在从大量候选轨迹中直接选择最优轨迹时难以优化，且扩散模型中的高斯扰动常产生不现实的轨迹；同时，现有的强化学习训练方法缺乏结构化奖励信号，限制了优化效果。

Method: 提出HAD框架，包含分层扩散策略、结构保持轨迹扩展机制以及度量解耦策略优化（MDPO），实现更真实的轨迹生成和多目标强化学习优化。

Result: 在NAVSIM和HUGSIM上进行了广泛实验，HAD分别取得了+2.3 EPDMS和+4.9路线完成率的提升，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: HAD通过分层扩散与结构化强化学习优化，在端到端自动驾驶规划中实现了新的最先进性能。

Abstract: End-to-end planning has emerged as a dominant paradigm for autonomous driving, where recent models often adopt a scoring-selection framework to choose trajectories from a large set of candidates, with diffusion-based decoding showing strong promise. However, directly selecting from the entire candidate space remains difficult to optimize, and Gaussian perturbations used in diffusion often introduce unrealistic trajectories that complicate the denoising process. In addition, for training these models, reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise, but existing end-to-end RL approaches typically rely on a single coupled reward without structured signals, limiting optimization effectiveness. To address these challenges, we propose HAD, an end-to-end planning framework with a Hierarchical Diffusion Policy that decomposes planning into a coarse-to-fine process. To improve trajectory generation, we introduce Structure-Preserved Trajectory Expansion, which produces realistic candidates while maintaining kinematic structure. For policy learning, we develop Metric-Decoupled Policy Optimization (MDPO) to enable structured RL optimization across multiple driving objectives. Extensive experiments show that HAD achieves new state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM and HUGSIM, outperforming prior arts by a huge margin: +2.3 EPDMS on NAVSIM and +4.9 Route Completion on HUGSIM.

</details>


### [218] [Human-Robot Copilot for Data-Efficient Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03613)
*Rui Yan,Zaitian Gongye,Lars Paulsen,Xuxin Cheng,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Human-Robot Copilot的框架，通过引入缩放因子实现灵巧遥操作，在保证与多种机械臂兼容的同时提升了策略性能和数据收集效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于HG-DAgger范式的人在环路模仿学习方法难以兼顾灵巧性与通用性，限制了在有限演示下的策略鲁棒性。

Method: 设计了一个可调节的缩放因子机制，使人类能在策略执行中进行间歇性精细干预，同时适配不同结构的机械臂。

Result: 实验表明，相同数量的演示轨迹下，该框架比现有方法性能更高，且数据收集更高效。

Conclusion: Human-Robot Copilot框架有效平衡了灵巧性与通用性，提高了机器人技能学习的数据利用效率和实用性。

Abstract: Collecting human demonstrations via teleoperation is a common approach for teaching robots task-specific skills. However, when only a limited number of demonstrations are available, policies are prone to entering out-of-distribution (OOD) states due to compounding errors or environmental stochasticity. Existing interactive imitation learning or human-in-the-loop methods try to address this issue by following the Human-Gated DAgger (HG-DAgger) paradigm, an approach that augments demonstrations through selective human intervention during policy execution. Nevertheless, these approaches struggle to balance dexterity and generality: they either provide fine-grained corrections but are limited to specific kinematic structures, or achieve generality at the cost of precise control. To overcome this limitation, we propose the Human-Robot Copilot framework that can leverage a scaling factor for dexterous teleoperation while maintaining compatibility with a wide range of industrial and research manipulators. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework achieves higher performance with the same number of demonstration trajectories. Moreover, since corrective interventions are required only intermittently, the overall data collection process is more efficient and less time-consuming.

</details>


### [219] [Towards Edge Intelligence via Autonomous Navigation: A Robot-Assisted Data Collection Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03623)
*Tingting Huang,Yingyang Chen,Sixian Qin,Zhijian Lin,Jun Li,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通信与学习双驱动的自主导航方案（CLD），用于提升边缘智能系统中移动机器人在复杂非视距环境下的数据采集性能，实现了导航、通信与学习的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人辅助数据采集方法在非视距（NLoS）等复杂环境中难以实现可靠高效的性能，亟需一种能够协同优化导航、通信与学习的自主决策机制。

Method: 提出通信与学习双驱动（CLD）自主导航框架，结合区域感知传播特性与非点质量机器人表示，并采用基于MM（majorization-minimization）的高效算法求解该非凸非光滑优化问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方案在避障导航、数据采集和模型训练方面均优于基准方法，并能通过调节权重因子灵活适应不同场景。

Conclusion: CLD方案有效实现了机器人在复杂环境中的高效数据采集与学习性能提升，具备良好的适应性与应用前景。

Abstract: With the growing demand for large-scale and high-quality data in edge intelligence systems, mobile robots are increasingly deployed to collect data proactively, particularly in complex environments. However, existing robot-assisted data collection methods face significant challenges in achieving reliable and efficient performance, especially in non-line-of-sight (NLoS) environments. This paper proposes a communication-and-learning dual-driven (CLD) autonomous navigation scheme that incorporates region-aware propagation characteristics and a non-point-mass robot representation. This scheme enables simultaneous optimization of navigation, communication, and learning performance. An efficient algorithm based on majorization-minimization (MM) is proposed to solve the non-convex and non-smooth CLD problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves superior performance in collision-avoidance navigation, data collection, and model training compared to benchmark methods. It is also shown that CLD can adapt to different scenarios by flexibly adjusting the weight factor among navigation, communication and learning objectives.

</details>


### [220] [A Multi-View 3D Telepresence System for XR Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03730)
*Enes Ulas Dincer,Manuel Zaremski,Alexandra Nick,Elias Wucher,Barbara Deml,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合多视角几何与腕部RGB流的VR遥操作可视化系统，显著提升了机器人遥操作中的深度感知与任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于屏幕的界面难以提供有效的3D深度线索，限制了机器人遥操作的直观性和效率，因此需要更沉浸、精确的可视化方案。

Method: 构建了一个多视角VR临场感系统，融合三个相机的几何信息生成GPU加速的点云，并在独立VR设备上渲染；同时集成腕部RGB摄像头以补充局部高分辨率细节。

Result: 在31名参与者上的实验表明，该系统在任务成功率、完成时间、主观工作负荷和可用性方面均优于RGB流、直接立体视觉投影和其他点云方法。点云模态即使无RGB辅助也优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 结合全局3D结构与局部高分辨率视觉细节能显著提升遥操作体验，为下一代机器人遥操作系统提供了可靠的基础。

Abstract: Robot teleoperation is critical for applications such as remote maintenance, fleet robotics, search and rescue, and data collection for robot learning. Effective teleoperation requires intuitive 3D visualization with reliable depth cues, which conventional screen-based interfaces often fail to provide. We introduce a multi-view VR telepresence system that (1) fuses geometry from three cameras to produce GPU-accelerated point-cloud rendering on standalone VR hardware, and (2) integrates a wrist-mounted RGB stream to provide high-resolution local detail where point-cloud accuracy is limited. Our pipeline supports real-time rendering of approximately 75k points on the Meta Quest 3. A within-subject study was conducted with 31 participants to compare our system to other visualisation modalities, such as RGB streams, a projection of stereo-vision directly in the VR device and point clouds without providing additional RGB information. Across three different teleoperated manipulation tasks, we measured task success, completion time, perceived workload, and usability. Our system achieved the best overall performance, while the Point Cloud modality without RGB also outperforming the RGB streams and OpenTeleVision. These results show that combining global 3D structure with localized high-resolution detail substantially improves telepresence for manipulation and provides a strong foundation for next-generation robot teleoperation systems.

</details>


### [221] [CT-VoxelMap: Efficient Continuous-Time LiDAR-Inertial Odometry with Probabilistic Adaptive Voxel Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03747)
*Lei Zhao,Xingyi Li,Tianchen Deng,Chuan Cao,Han Zhang,Weidong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于B样条和李群增量表示的连续时间位姿估计方法，用于提升移动机器人在快速运动或复杂地形下的定位稳定性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于B样条的连续时间位姿估计方法在优化控制点或四元数空间中建模时存在雅可比推导复杂、拟合误差被忽略等问题，限制了定位精度与效率。

Method: 将控制点的增量表示定义在矩阵李群上作为优化变量，利用B样条的累积特性简化雅可比计算；结合IMU前向传播信息在线估计拟合误差，并采用混合特征体素地图管理策略；引入重估计策略提升计算效率与鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上验证了方法的优越性能，在多数序列中表现优于现有方法，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过李群增量参数化、在线拟合误差补偿和高效重估计策略，显著提升了连续时间位姿估计的精度、鲁棒性与计算效率。

Abstract: Maintaining stable and accurate localization during fast motion or on rough terrain remains highly challenging for mobile robots with onboard resources. Currently, multi-sensor fusion methods based on continuous-time representation offer a potential and effective solution to this challenge. Among these, spline-based methods provide an efficient and intuitive approach for continuous-time representation. Previous continuous-time odometry works based on B-splines either treat control points as variables to be estimated or perform estimation in quaternion space, which introduces complexity in deriving analytical Jacobians and often overlooks the fitting error between the spline and the true trajectory over time. To address these issues, we first propose representing the increments of control points on matrix Lie groups as variables to be estimated. Leveraging the feature of the cumulative form of B-splines, we derive a more compact formulation that yields simpler analytical Jacobians without requiring additional boundary condition considerations. Second, we utilize forward propagation information from IMU measurements to estimate fitting errors online and further introduce a hybrid feature-based voxel map management strategy, enhancing system accuracy and robustness. Finally, we propose a re-estimation policy that significantly improves system computational efficiency and robustness. The proposed method is evaluated on multiple challenging public datasets, demonstrating superior performance on most sequences. Detailed ablation studies are conducted to analyze the impact of each module on the overall pose estimation system.

</details>


### [222] [Build on Priors: Vision--Language--Guided Neuro-Symbolic Imitation Learning for Data-Efficient Real-World Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03759)
*Pierrick Lorang,Johannes Huemer,Timothy Duggan,Kai Goebel,Patrik Zips,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可扩展的神经符号框架，能从少量未标注技能演示中自动构建符号规划域和数据高效控制策略，无需人工领域工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法依赖手工抽象、语义标注轨迹或大量演示数据，限制了可扩展性和实际应用。目标是实现仅用极少演示即可学习长视野操作任务。

Method: 通过视觉语言模型（VLM）对技能进行分类并识别等价高层状态，构建状态转移图；利用Answer Set Programming求解器生成PDDL规划域；设计oracle函数提取每个技能策略的任务相关观测与动作空间；在控制参考层面而非原始执行信号层面学习策略，并利用已知控制器通过单次演示投影到其他物体进行数据增强。

Result: 在真实工业叉车和Kinova Gen3机械臂上验证，结果表明该框架能在极少量演示下（1-30次）实现高效学习，并具备跨平台泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将控制学习、VLM驱动的抽象与自动化规划综合成统一管道，为可扩展、数据高效、无需专家干预且可解释的神经符号机器人提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Enabling robots to learn long-horizon manipulation tasks from a handful of demonstrations remains a central challenge in robotics. Existing neuro-symbolic approaches often rely on hand-crafted symbolic abstractions, semantically labeled trajectories or large demonstration datasets, limiting their scalability and real-world applicability. We present a scalable neuro-symbolic framework that autonomously constructs symbolic planning domains and data-efficient control policies from as few as one to thirty unannotated skill demonstrations, without requiring manual domain engineering. Our method segments demonstrations into skills and employs a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to classify skills and identify equivalent high-level states, enabling automatic construction of a state-transition graph. This graph is processed by an Answer Set Programming solver to synthesize a PDDL planning domain, which an oracle function exploits to isolate the minimal, task-relevant and target relative observation and action spaces for each skill policy. Policies are learned at the control reference level rather than at the raw actuator signal level, yielding a smoother and less noisy learning target. Known controllers can be leveraged for real-world data augmentation by projecting a single demonstration onto other objects in the scene, simultaneously enriching the graph construction process and the dataset for imitation learning. We validate our framework primarily on a real industrial forklift across statistically rigorous manipulation trials, and demonstrate cross-platform generality on a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm across two standard benchmarks. Our results show that grounding control learning, VLM-driven abstraction, and automated planning synthesis into a unified pipeline constitutes a practical path toward scalable, data-efficient, expert-free and interpretable neuro-symbolic robotics.

</details>


### [223] [A Novel Hybrid PID-LQR Controller for Sit-To-Stand Assistance Using a CAD-Integrated Simscape Multibody Lower Limb Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03766)
*Ranjeet Kumbhar,Rajmeet Singh,Appaso M Gadade,Ashish Singla,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于下肢外骨骼站起转移控制的混合PID-LQR控制器，通过高保真动力学模型和生理轨迹仿真验证其在跟踪精度、稳定时间和超调等方面的性能显著优于传统PID和LQR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人体-外骨骼系统的高度非线性和时变动力学特性，以及临床安全对轨迹跟踪的严格要求，实现精确的站起转移控制仍是康复机器人领域的核心挑战。

Method: 构建基于SolidWorks CAD模型导入MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Multibody的高保真动力学模型，利用OpenSim生成符合生理特征的髋、膝、踝关节参考轨迹，并将其分为三个生物力学阶段；设计并比较PID、LQR及混合PID-LQR三种控制策略，其中混合控制器通过调节系数alpha=0.65融合LQR的最优瞬态响应与PID的积分抗干扰能力。

Result: 混合PID-LQR控制器相较于PID在髋关节和膝关节的RMSE分别降低72.3%和70.4%，所有关节的调节时间减少超过90%，超调控制在2.39%-6.10%之间，在各项指标上均优于两种基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合PID-LQR控制器在站起转移任务中表现出优越的控制性能和鲁棒性，具备良好的临床应用前景，可为助行外骨骼系统的实际部署提供技术支持。

Abstract: Precise control of lower limb exoskeletons during sit-to-stand (STS) transitions remains a central challenge in rehabilitation robotics owing to the highly nonlinear, time-varying dynamics of the human-exoskeleton system and the stringent trajectory tracking requirements imposed by clinical safety. This paper presents the systematic design, simulation, and comparative evaluation of three control strategies: a classical Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controller, a Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR), and a novel Hybrid PID-LQR controller applied to a bilateral lower limb exoskeleton performing the sit-to-stand transition. A high-fidelity, physics-based dynamic model of the exoskeleton is constructed by importing a SolidWorks CAD assembly directly into the MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Multibody environment, preserving accurate geometric and inertial properties of all links. Physiologically representative reference joint trajectories for the hip, knee, and ankle joints are generated using OpenSim musculoskeletal simulation and decomposed into three biomechanical phases: flexion-momentum (0-33%), momentum-transfer (34-66%), and extension (67-100%). The proposed Hybrid PID-LQR controller combines the optimal transient response of LQR with the integral disturbance rejection of PID through a tuned blending coefficient alpha = 0.65. Simulation results demonstrate that the Hybrid PID-LQR achieves RMSE reductions of 72.3% and 70.4% over PID at the hip and knee joints, respectively, reduces settling time by over 90% relative to PID across all joints, and limits overshoot to 2.39%-6.10%, confirming its superiority over both baseline strategies across all evaluated performance metrics and demonstrating strong translational potential for clinical assistive exoskeleton deployment.

</details>


### [224] [OpenRC: An Open-Source Robotic Colonoscopy Framework for Multimodal Data Acquisition and Autonomy Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03781)
*Siddhartha Kapuria,Mohammad Rafiee Javazm,Naruhiko Ikoma,Joga Ivatury,Mohammad Ali Nasseri,Nassir Navab,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenRC是一个开源模块化机器人结肠镜框架，可改装传统内窥镜，支持视频、操作指令、执行状态和远端姿态的同步记录，旨在推动多模态机器人结肠镜与手术自主化的可重复研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结肠镜平台缺乏对操作控制、器械运动与视觉反馈耦合动态的系统性研究支持，限制了机器人结肠镜、医学成像及视觉-语言-动作学习等领域的可重复闭环研究。

Method: 开发了一个名为OpenRC的开源模块化机器人结肠镜框架，兼容常规内窥镜并保持临床流程不变，支持多模态数据同步采集，并通过实验验证运动一致性及跨模态感知延迟。

Result: 成功收集包含1,894次遥操作片段（约19小时）的多模态数据集，涵盖10种结构化任务变体，包括常规导航、失败事件与恢复行为。

Conclusion: OpenRC通过整合开放硬件与对齐的多模态数据集，为多模态机器人结肠镜与手术自主化研究提供了可复制的技术基础。

Abstract: Colorectal cancer screening critically depends on colonoscopy, yet existing platforms offer limited support for systematically studying the coupled dynamics of operator control, instrument motion, and visual feedback. This gap restricts reproducible closed-loop research in robotic colonoscopy, medical imaging, and emerging vision-language-action (VLA) learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we present OpenRC, an open-source modular robotic colonoscopy framework that retrofits conventional scopes while preserving clinical workflow. The framework supports simultaneous recording of video, operator commands, actuation state, and distal tip pose. We experimentally validated motion consistency and quantified cross-modal latency across sensing streams. Using this platform, we collected a multimodal dataset comprising 1,894 teleoperated episodes ~19 hours across 10 structured task variations of routine navigation, failure events, and recovery behaviors. By unifying open hardware and an aligned multimodal dataset, OpenRC provides a reproducible foundation for research in multimodal robotic colonoscopy and surgical autonomy.

</details>


### [225] [DC-Ada: Reward-Only Decentralized Observation-Interface Adaptation for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03905)
*Saad Alqithami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DC-Ada是一种无需梯度、通信最少的去中心化适应方法，通过调整每个机器人的观测变换来适应多机器人系统中的感知异质性，保持预训练共享策略冻结，在严重覆盖型任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制器在传感器配置不同的机器人上部署时性能显著下降，即使任务和动作接口相同。需要一种能够在部署时适应感知异质性的方法，以提升多机器人团队的实际应用能力。

Method: 提出DC-Ada方法，保持预训练的共享策略冻结，学习紧凑的、每个机器人专属的观测变换，将异质感知映射到固定的推理接口；采用基于接受/拒绝的随机搜索（梯度-free）和短时间的公共随机rollout进行优化，仅依赖标量团队回报且无需持续通信。

Result: 在2D多机器人模拟器中评估了四种异质性场景和四个基准，结果表明异质性会显著降低冻结共享策略的性能；不同方法在不同任务中表现各异：观测归一化在仓库物流中表现最好，而DC-Ada在严重覆盖型地图构建任务中对任务完成度提升最明显。

Conclusion: DC-Ada提供了一种实用的部署时适应方案，适用于感知异质的多机器人团队，尤其在无需策略微调和有限通信的场景下具有优势。

Abstract: Heterogeneity is a defining feature of deployed multi-robot teams: platforms often differ in sensing modalities, ranges, fields of view, and failure patterns. Controllers trained under nominal sensing can degrade sharply when deployed on robots with missing or mismatched sensors, even when the task and action interface are unchanged. We present DC-Ada, a reward-only decentralized adaptation method that keeps a pretrained shared policy frozen and instead adapts compact per-robot observation transforms to map heterogeneous sensing into a fixed inference interface. DC-Ada is gradient-free and communication-minimal: it uses budgeted accept/reject random search with short common-random-number rollouts under a strict step budget. We evaluate DC-Ada against four baselines in a deterministic 2D multi-robot simulator covering warehouse logistics, search and rescue, and collaborative mapping, across four heterogeneity regimes (H0--H3) and five seeds with a matched budget of $200{,}000$ joint environment steps per run. Results show that heterogeneity can substantially degrade a frozen shared policy and that no single mitigation dominates across all tasks and metrics. Observation normalization is strongest for reward robustness in warehouse logistics and competitive in search and rescue, while the frozen shared policy is strongest for reward in collaborative mapping. DC-Ada offers a useful complementary operating point: it improves completion most clearly in severe coverage-based mapping while requiring only scalar team returns and no policy fine-tuning or persistent communication. These results position DC-Ada as a practical deploy-time adaptation method for heterogeneous teams.

</details>


### [226] [VA-FastNavi-MARL: Real-Time Robot Control with Multimedia-Driven Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03998)
*Yang Zhang,Shengxi Jing,Fengxiang Wang,Yuan Feng,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VA-FastNavi-MARL 是一种用于人机交互的框架，能够将异步的视听输入对齐为统一的潜在表示，并通过元强化学习实现对未见指令的快速适应，具有低延迟和高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态、异构的多媒体指令下实现低延迟、实时响应的人机交互面临挑战，尤其是传统方法受限于繁重的感官处理和对新指令的适应能力不足。

Method: 提出 VA-FastNavi-MARL 框架，通过元强化学习将多样化指令视为可导航目标的分布，将异步音频-视觉输入融合到统一的潜在空间中，采用模态无关的数据流以实现低延迟控制。

Result: 在多臂工作区上的实验表明，该方法在样本效率上显著优于基线方法，并能在噪声多媒体流下保持稳健的实时执行性能。

Conclusion: VA-FastNavi-MARL 实现了高效、低延迟的多模态指令理解与导航控制，适用于复杂、动态的人机交互环境。

Abstract: Interpreting dynamic, heterogeneous multimedia commands with real-time responsiveness is critical for Human-Robot Interaction. We present VA-FastNavi-MARL, a framework that aligns asynchronous audio-visual inputs into a unified latent representation. By treating diverse instructions as a distribution of navigable goals via Meta-Reinforcement Learning, our method enables rapid adaptation to unseen directives with negligible inference overhead. Unlike approaches bottlenecked by heavy sensory processing, our modality-agnostic stream ensures seamless, low-latency control. Validation on a multi-arm workspace confirms that VA-FastNavi-MARL significantly outperforms baselines in sample efficiency and maintains robust, real-time execution even under noisy multimedia streams.

</details>


### [227] [Dynamic Whole-Body Dancing with Humanoid Robots -- A Model-Based Control Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03999)
*Shibowen Zhang,Jiayang Wu,Guannan Liu,Helin Zhu,Junjie Liu,Zhehan Li,Junhong Guo,Xiaokun Leng,Hangxin Liu,Jingwen Zhang,Jikai Wang,Zonghai Chen,Zhicheng He,Jiayi Wang,Yao Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的框架，用于在人形机器人上生成和执行动态全身舞蹈动作，分为离线生成和在线执行两个阶段，并通过预测控制实现鲁棒性与动态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使人形机器人能够在真实环境中稳定、动态地执行复杂的舞蹈动作，克服传统方法在动态适应性和实时性方面的不足。

Method: 离线阶段利用运动捕捉数据，通过二次规划和轨迹优化生成动态可行的舞蹈动作；在线阶段采用基于质心动力学的模型预测控制，实时跟踪动作并调整脚步以应对扰动。

Result: 在Kuavo 4Pro机器人上验证了该框架的有效性，实现了四分钟的公开舞蹈表演，并表明更长的预测时域可提升动作表现力与执行稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段框架结合未来状态预测，能够有效实现人形机器人动态、鲁棒的舞蹈运动生成与执行。

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated model-based framework for generating and executing dynamic whole-body dance motions on humanoid robots. The framework operates in two stages: offline motion generation and online motion execution, both leveraging future state prediction to enable robust and dynamic dance motions in real-world environments. In the offline motion generation stage, human dance demonstrations are captured via a motion capture (MoCap) system, retargeted to the robot by solving a Quadratic Programming (QP) problem, and further refined using Trajectory Optimization (TO) to ensure dynamic feasibility. In the online motion execution stage, a centroidal dynamics-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework tracks the planned motions in real time and proactively adjusts swing foot placement to adapt to real world disturbances. We validate our framework on the full-size humanoid robot Kuavo 4Pro, demonstrating the dynamic dance motions both in simulation and in a four-minute live public performance with a team of four robots. Experimental results show that longer prediction horizons improve both motion expressiveness in planning and stability in execution.

</details>


### [228] [Adaptive Action Chunking at Inference-time for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04161)
*Yuanchang Liang,Xiaobo Wang,Kai Wang,Shuo Wang,Xiaojiang Peng,Haoyu Chen,David Kim Huat Chua,Prahlad Vadakkepat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于动作熵的自适应动作分块（AAC）策略，以动态调整视觉-语言-动作模型中的动作执行长度，从而在响应性和一致性之间取得平衡，并在多种机器人操作任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型通常使用固定的动作分块大小，难以在响应新信息和保持动作连贯性之间取得平衡，限制了其在多样化操作任务中的性能和可扩展性。

Method: 提出利用动作熵作为判断依据，动态决定每个动作块的大小，实现自适应分块（AAC），使模型能根据当前预测自动调整执行长度。

Result: 在多个模拟和真实世界机器人操作任务上的实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法，提升了整体性能。

Conclusion: 自适应动作分块策略有效解决了固定分块带来的响应性与连续性之间的矛盾，增强了视觉-语言-动作模型在复杂任务中的适应能力与实用性。

Abstract: In Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, action chunking (i.e., executing a sequence of actions without intermediate replanning) is a key technique to improve robotic manipulation abilities. However, a large chunk size reduces the model's responsiveness to new information, while a small one increases the likelihood of mode-jumping, jerky behavior resulting from discontinuities between chunks. Therefore, selecting the optimal chunk size is an urgent demand to balance the model's reactivity and consistency. Unfortunately, a dominant trend in current VLA models is an empirical fixed chunk length at inference-time, hindering their superiority and scalability across diverse manipulation tasks. To address this issue, we propose a novel Adaptive Action Chunking (AAC) strategy, which exploits action entropy as the cue to adaptively determine the chunk size based on current predictions. Extensive experiments on a wide range of simulated and real-world robotic manipulation tasks have demonstrated that our approach substantially improves performance over the state-of-the-art alternatives. The videos and source code are publicly available at https://lance-lot.github.io/adaptive-chunking.github.io/.

</details>


### [229] [Primitive-based Truncated Diffusion for Efficient Trajectory Generation of Differential Drive Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04166)
*Long Xu,Choilam Wong,Yuhang Zhong,Junxiao Lin,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种学习增强的运动规划方法，用于差动驱动移动 manipulators，通过关键点序列提取和基于原语的截断扩散模型提升效率、成功率和路径多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高移动 manipulators 在复杂环境中的运动规划效率、成功率和路径优化能力，克服传统方法在高维空间中采样效率低和路径多样性不足的问题。
